 _whitby_

 

 one greatest and suddenest storms record been

 experienced here results both strange and unique weather had

 somewhat sultry any degree uncommon month of

 august saturday evening fine ever known and great

 body holiday-makers laid out yesterday visits mulgrave woods

 robin hood’s bay rig mill runswick staithes and various trips in

 neighbourhood whitby steamers _emma_ and _scarborough_ made

 trips up and down coast and unusual amount of

 “tripping” both and whitby day unusually fine till the

 afternoon some gossips frequent east cliff

 churchyard and commanding eminence watch wide sweep of

 sea visible north and east called attention sudden show of

 “mares’-tails” high sky north-west wind then

 blowing south-west mild degree barometrical

 language ranked “no 2 light breeze” coastguard duty once

 made report and one old fisherman more than half century has

 kept watch weather signs east cliff foretold emphatic

 manner coming sudden storm approach sunset so very

 beautiful so grand masses splendidly-coloured clouds that

 quite assemblage walk along cliff old

 churchyard enjoy beauty sun dipped below black

 mass kettleness standing boldly athwart western sky its

 downward way marked myriad clouds every sunset-colour--flame

 purple pink green violet and all tints gold and

 masses large seemingly absolute blackness all

 sorts shapes well outlined colossal silhouettes the

 experience lost painters and doubtless some the

 sketches “prelude great storm” grace r a and r

 i walls next more than one captain made up mind and

 “cobble” “mule” term different

 classes boats remain harbour till storm passed

 wind fell away entirely during evening and midnight there

 dead calm sultry heat and prevailing intensity which on

 approach thunder affects persons sensitive nature there

 few lights sight sea even coasting steamers

 usually “hug” shore so closely kept well seaward and but

 few fishing-boats sight only sail noticeable foreign

 schooner all sails set seemingly going westwards the

 foolhardiness ignorance officers prolific theme for

 comment whilst remained sight and efforts made signal

 reduce sail face danger night shut down she

 seen sails idly flapping gently rolled undulating

 swell sea

 

 “as idle painted ship upon painted ocean”

 

 shortly ten o’clock stillness air grew quite

 oppressive and silence so marked bleating sheep

 inland barking dog town distinctly heard and the

 band pier lively french air like discord the

 great harmony nature’s silence little midnight came a

 strange sound sea and high overhead air began to

 carry strange faint hollow booming

 

 without warning tempest broke rapidity which the

 time seemed incredible and even afterwards impossible realize

 whole aspect nature once became convulsed waves rose in

 growing fury each overtopping fellow till few minutes

 lately glassy sea like roaring and devouring monster

 white-crested waves beat madly level sands and rushed up the

 shelving cliffs others broke piers and spume swept

 lanthorns lighthouses rise end either pier

 whitby harbour wind roared like thunder and blew such

 force difficulty even strong men kept feet

 clung grim clasp iron stanchions found necessary

 clear entire piers mass onlookers else the

 fatalities night increased manifold add to

 difficulties and dangers time masses sea-fog came

 drifting inland--white wet clouds swept ghostly fashion

 so dank and damp and cold needed little effort of

 imagination think spirits lost sea were

 touching living brethren clammy hands death and many

 one shuddered wreaths sea-mist swept by times mist

 cleared and sea some distance seen glare the

 lightning now came thick and fast followed such sudden peals

 thunder whole sky overhead seemed trembling under shock

 footsteps storm

 

 some scenes thus revealed immeasurable grandeur and of

 absorbing interest--the sea running mountains high threw skywards with

 each wave mighty masses white foam tempest seemed to

 snatch and whirl away space and fishing-boat with

 rag sail running madly shelter blast now and again

 white wings storm-tossed sea-bird summit east

 cliff new searchlight ready experiment yet been

 tried officers charge got working order and in

 pauses inrushing mist swept surface sea

 once twice service most effective fishing-boat

 gunwale under water rushed harbour able guidance

 sheltering light avoid danger dashing against the

 piers each boat achieved safety port shout of

 joy mass people shore shout moment seemed

 cleave gale and swept away rush

 

 long searchlight discovered some distance away schooner

 all sails set apparently same vessel noticed

 earlier evening wind time backed east

 and shudder amongst watchers cliff they

 realized terrible danger now was and the

 port lay great flat reef so many good ships time

 time suffered and wind blowing present quarter

 quite impossible fetch entrance the

 harbour now nearly hour high tide waves so

 great troughs shallows shore almost

 visible and schooner all sails set rushing such

 only hell” came another rush sea-fog greater than

 like grey pall and left available men only organ hearing

 than before rays searchlight kept fixed harbour

 piers leaping wave wave rushed headlong speed

 swept strange schooner blast all sail set and

 gained safety harbour searchlight followed her and a

 shudder ran through all saw her lashed helm a

 corpse drooping head swung horribly and fro each

 motion ship other form seen deck all great

 awe came all realised ship miracle had

 found harbour unsteered save hand dead man however

 all took place more quickly than takes write words the

 accumulation sand and gravel washed many tides and many

 course considerable concussion vessel drove up on

 “top-hammer” came crashing down but strangest all instant

 6 july finished taking cargo silver sand and boxes earth

 noon set sail east wind fresh crew five hands two mates

 11 july dawn entered bosphorus boarded turkish customs

 guarding squadron backsheesh again work officers thorough but

 quick want off soon dark passed archipelago

 seemed scared speak out

 

 

 

 14 july somewhat anxious crew men all steady fellows who

 sailed before mate make out wrong only

 told _something_ and crossed themselves mate lost temper

 one day and struck him expected fierce quarrel but

 all quiet

 

 

 

 16 july mate reported morning one crew petrofsky was

 missing account it took larboard watch eight bells last

 night relieved abramoff go bunk men more

 downcast than ever all said expected something kind but

 say more than _something_ aboard mate getting very

 impatient them feared some trouble ahead

 

 

 

 17 july yesterday one men olgaren came cabin and in

 awestruck way confided thought strange man

 aboard ship said watch sheltering

 behind deck-house rain-storm saw tall

 thin man like any crew come up companion-way

 and go along deck forward and disappear followed cautiously

 got bows found one and hatchways all closed

 panic superstitious fear and afraid panic may

 spread allay it to-day search entire ship carefully from

 stem stern

 

 

 

 later day got together whole crew and told them they

 evidently thought some one ship search from

 stem stern first mate angry said folly and yield such

 foolish ideas demoralise men said engage keep

 rest began thorough search all keeping abreast lanterns left

 corner unsearched only big wooden boxes there

 odd corners man hide men much relieved when

 search over and went back work cheerfully first mate scowled but

 said nothing

 

 

 

 _22 july_--rough weather last three days and all hands busy with

 sails--no time frightened men seem forgotten dread

 mate cheerful again and all good terms praised men work bad

 weather passed gibralter and out through straits all well

 

 

 

 _24 july_--there seems some doom ship already hand short

 and entering bay biscay wild weather ahead and yet last

 night another man lost--disappeared like first came off his

 watch and seen again men all panic fear sent round

 robin asking double watch fear alone mate

 angry fear some trouble either men do

 _28 july_--four days hell knocking sort maelstrom

 and wind tempest sleep any one men all worn out hardly

 know set watch since one fit go on second mate

 volunteered steer and watch and let men snatch few hours’ sleep

 wind abating seas still terrific feel less ship is

 _29 july_--another tragedy single watch to-night crew too

 tired double morning watch came deck find one

 except steersman raised outcry and all came deck thorough search

 one found now without second mate and crew panic mate

 and agreed go armed henceforth and wait any sign cause

 

 

 

 _30 july_--last night rejoiced nearing england weather fine

 all sails set retired worn out slept soundly awaked mate telling

 both man watch and steersman missing only self and mate and

 two hands left work ship

 

 

 

 _1 august_--two days fog and sail sighted hoped in

 english channel able signal help get somewhere

 having power work sails run wind dare lower

 raise again seem drifting some terrible

 doom mate now more demoralised than either men stronger nature

 seems worked inwardly against himself men beyond fear

 working stolidly and patiently minds made up worst are

 russian roumanian

 

 

 

 _2 august midnight_--woke up few minutes’ sleep hearing cry

 seemingly outside port see nothing fog rushed deck and

 watch one more gone lord help us mate says past straits

 dover moment fog lifting saw north foreland as

 heard man cry out so now off north sea and

 only god guide fog seems move us and god

 seems deserted us

 see work helm” and warning look and finger his

 _dr van helsing’s memorandum_

 

 _5 november afternoon_--i least sane thank god mercy

 all events though proving dreadful left

 madam mina sleeping within holy circle took way castle

 blacksmith hammer took carriage veresti was

 useful though doors all open broke off rusty

 hinges lest some ill-intent ill-chance close them so that

 entered get out jonathan’s bitter experience served

 here memory diary found way old chapel i

 knew work lay air oppressive seemed if

 some sulphurous fume times made dizzy either

 roaring ears heard afar off howl wolves

 bethought dear madam mina and terrible plight

 dilemma horns

 

 her dare take place left safe the

 vampire holy circle and yet even wolf i

 resolve work lay here and wolves must

 submit god’s will any rate only death and

 freedom beyond so choose her the

 choice easy maw wolf better rest than

 grave vampire so make choice go work

 

 knew least three graves find--graves are

 inhabit so search and search and find one them lay her

 vampire sleep so full life and voluptuous beauty shudder as

 though come murder ah doubt old time when

 such things were many man set forth such task mine

 found last heart fail him and nerve so delay

 and delay and delay till mere beauty and fascination the

 wanton un-dead hypnotise him and remain and on till sunset

 come and vampire sleep over beautiful eyes fair

 woman open and look love and voluptuous mouth present a

 kiss--and man weak and remain one more victim vampire

 fold one more swell grim and grisly ranks un-dead

 

 some fascination surely moved mere presence

 such one even lying lay tomb fretted age and

 heavy dust centuries though horrid odour such

 lairs count had yes moved--i van helsing

 all purpose and motive hate--i moved a

 yearning delay seemed paralyse faculties and clog my

 soul need natural sleep and the

 strange oppression air beginning overcome me certain it

 lapsing sleep open-eyed sleep one yields

 sweet fascination came through snow-stilled air a

 long low wail so full woe and pity woke like sound

 clarion voice dear madam mina heard

 

 braced again horrid task and found wrenching

 away tomb-tops one other sisters other dark one dared not

 pause look sister lest once more should

 begin enthrall go searching until presently find in

 high great tomb made one much beloved other fair sister

 which like jonathan seen gather out atoms of

 mist so fair look on so radiantly beautiful so

 exquisitely voluptuous instinct man me calls

 some sex love and protect one hers made head whirl

 new emotion god thanked soul-wail dear madam

 mina died out ears and spell wrought

 further upon me nerved wild work time had

 searched all tombs chapel so far tell and as

 only three un-dead phantoms around the

 night took more active un-dead existent

 one great tomb more lordly than all rest huge was and

 nobly proportioned one word

 

 dracula

 won gone further butchery have

 endured horrid screeching stake drove home plunging of

 _mina harker’s journal_

 

 _6 november_--it late afternoon professor and i

 took way towards east whence knew jonathan coming did

 go fast though way steeply downhill take

 heavy rugs and wraps us dared face possibility being

 left without warmth cold and snow take some our

 provisions too perfect desolation and so far we

 see through snowfall even sign of

 habitation gone mile tired heavy

 walking and sat down rest looked back and saw the

 clear line dracula’s castle cut sky so deep under

 hill whereon set angle perspective the

 carpathian mountains far below it saw all grandeur

 perched thousand feet summit sheer precipice and with

 seemingly great gap and steep adjacent mountain

 any side something wild and uncanny place we

 hear distant howling wolves far off the

 sound even though coming muffled through deadening snowfall was

 full terror knew way dr van helsing searching about

 trying seek some strategic point less

 exposed case attack rough roadway still led downwards we

 trace through drifted snow

 

 little while professor signalled me so got up and joined

 him found wonderful spot sort natural hollow rock

 entrance like doorway two boulders took the

 hand and drew in “see” said “here shelter and

 wolves come meet one one” brought our

 furs and made snug nest me and got out some provisions and

 forced upon me eat even try so was

 repulsive me and much liked please him could

 bring attempt looked sad not

 reproach me taking field-glasses case stood top

 rock and began search horizon suddenly called out--

 strange see snow falling such heavy flakes close us

 and beyond sun shining more and more brightly sank down

 towards far mountain tops sweeping glass all around could

 see and dots moving singly and twos and threes and larger
